I want a quick word with him, too.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To terminate a call on (a telephone); to hang up."],"synonyms":[{"word":"hang up"}]},{"examples":[{"text":"I've put myself down for the new Spanish conversation course.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To add a name to a list."]},{"examples":[{"text":"BP are putting petrol and diesel down in what could be the start of a price war.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To make prices, or taxes, lower."],"tags":["Ireland","UK"]},{"examples":[{"text":"I had just put Mary down when you rang.
